concatenate all text runs inside the paragraph\n    t_elems = list(p_elem.iter(f'{HP}t'))\n    if not t_elems:\n        return False\n\n    original = ''.join([(t.text or '') for t in t_elems])\n    replaced = original\n    for k, v in replacements.items():\n        replaced = replaced.replace('{{' + k + '}}', v)\n\n    if replaced == original:\n        return False\n\n    # write back: keep structure but avoid split placeholders by collapsing into first <hp:t>\n    t_elems[0].text = replaced\n    for t in t_elems[1:]:\n        t.text = ''\n\n    # remove layout-cache elements within this paragraph\n    # In practice we see <hp:linesegarray> and <hp:lineseg>\n    # Remove any descendant whose localname matches these.\n    to_remove = []\n    for elem in list(p_elem.iter()):\n        ln = localname(elem.tag).lower()\n        if ln in ('linesegarray', 'lineseg', 'linesegarray', 'lineseg'):\n            if elem is not p_elem:\n                to_remove.append(elem)\n\n    # remove from parent (can't remove while iterating)\n    for elem in to_remove:\n        parent = None\n        # ElementTree doesn't provide getparent; walk to find parent.\n        for candidate in p_elem.iter():\n            for child in list(candidate):\n                if child is elem:\n                    parent = candidate\n                    break\n            if parent is not None:\n                break\n        if parent is not None:\n            parent.remove(elem)\n\n    return True\n\n\nwith zipfile.ZipFile(SRC, 'r') as zin, zipfile.ZipFile(DST, 'w', compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:\n    for info in zin.infolist():\n        raw = zin.read(info.filename)\n\n        if not info.filename.lower().endswith('.xml'):\n            zout.writestr(info, raw)\n            continue\n\n        # Try XML parse first\n        try:\n            root = ET.fromstring(raw)\n        except Exception:\n            # fallback: plain text replacement\n            try:\n                text = raw.decode('utf-8')\n            except Exception:\n                zout.writestr(info, raw)\n                continue\n\n            if '{{' in text:\n                for k, v in replacements.items():\n                    text = text.replace('{{' + k + '}}', v)\n            # We can't reliably remove lineSegArray without parsing; write replaced text anyway.\n            zout.writestr(info, text.encode('utf-8'))\n            continue\n\n        modified_any = False\n        for p in root.iter(f'{HP}p'):\n            if replace_in_paragraph(p):\n                modified_any = True\n\n        out_bytes = ET.tostring(root, encoding='utf-8', xml_declaration=True)\n\n        # Safety check: no placeholders should remain in XML we touched\n        if placeholder_re.search(out_bytes.decode('utf-8', errors='replace')):\n            # We'll still write, but emit a warning to stdout for debugging.\n            remaining = sorted(set(placeholder_re.findall(out_bytes.decode('utf-8', errors='replace'))))\n            print(f'WARNING: Remaining placeholders in {info.filename}: {remaining}')\n\n        zout.writestr(info, out_bytes)\n\nprint('Wrote:', DST)\nPY","description":"Generate filled HWPX by replacing placeholders and removing lineSegArray in modified paragraphs","timeout":120000,"run_in_background":false,"dangerouslyDisableSandbox":false}}],"model":"openai/gpt-5.2-20251211","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"OpenAI","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"c4b9c630-a0f2-4f29-ab00-1a41b33aad39","uuid":"b8bf345f-cf43-4e59-a1aa-d36c5d1a63b5"}
